# Best Speech Analysis — Project Goal and Resumption Status

Last corpus checkpoint: 2026-09-18. Provenance/report repair: 2026-09-17.

## Latest resumption update — Joe Biden victory speech review 395

Joe Biden’s victory speech (N_H7qoPLOYU) is complete: all 171 retained captions /
1,809 stored queue words were reread in one consecutive pass and recorded in
[full-context-address-279.md](writeups/full-context-address-279.md). Review 395
studies victory-to-stewardship, coalition credit, opponent-to-citizen reframing,
mandate-to-mechanism, choice-with-constraints, inflection-point analogy, value
repetition and grief-to-handoff. Electoral, public-health, historical, policy,
family and religious claims remain bounded or unverified; audiovisual delivery
was not reviewed. Current totals: 395 fully covered videos, 102,816 inspected
captions and 395 analytic reviews. The transcript queue is complete; the next
phase is the first validated pattern-card and practice-lesson slice.
The [corpus identity review workbook](research/corpus-identity-review-workbook.json)
covers all 395 reviewed queue items against the 480-row registry. It preserves
metadata and possible duplicate leads while requiring independent speaker,
event, upload-match and duplication decisions; it is blank.
Its validator enforces the identity/event-only boundary, and the
[summarizer](scripts/summarize_corpus_identity_reviews.py) preserves decision
distributions without treating them as recurrence evidence.
The [identity comparator](scripts/compare_corpus_identity_reviews.py) preserves
reviewer disagreements and requires adjudication before identity decisions can
affect recurrence denominators.

The [corpus pattern atlas](research/corpus-pattern-atlas.json) now indexes 394
eligible videos / 102,351 eligible caption segments with exact phrase-form
retrieval, candidate communication-job counts, and links back to evidence IDs.
One transcript is retained in the 395-video corpus but excluded from this atlas
under the explicit source-restriction policy. Atlas labels remain heuristic and
transcript-only until manual, audiovisual, independent, and learner checks are
completed. It now includes a stable 326/68 event-level split and a 192-item
stratified recurrence queue with a blank [independent transcript-function
workbook](research/corpus-recurrence-independent-review-workbook.json).
The [review request](writeups/corpus-recurrence-independent-review-request.md)
defines the independent procedure, controlled decisions, export validation and
interpretation boundary; no external review export has been received.
The recurrence validator preserves the recurrence-only/non-outcome boundary,
and the [recurrence comparator](scripts/compare_corpus_recurrence_reviews.py)
retains field-level disagreements and requires adjudication before any
denominator changes.
The [corpus atlas browser](writeups/corpus-pattern-atlas.html) exposes the same
retrieval layer across all 394 eligible videos with search, phrase-family and
candidate-function and event-split filters, exact candidate intervals and source
links. Candidate-function filtering remains heuristic and transcript-only.
The [exact phrase-form inventory](writeups/corpus-phrase-form-inventory.md)
now exposes 50 actual matched word forms across the restricted 102,351-caption
atlas, with train/holdout video counts, exact examples and bounded mechanism
hypotheses. Phrase recurrence remains heuristic and requires context and
independent review.
The separate [exact phrase-form independent-review workbook](research/corpus-phrase-form-independent-review-workbook.json)
now covers all 50 forms with sampled train/holdout contexts, exact-match,
function, event-identity, duplication-risk and recurrence decisions. It is
blank; no independent form judgments have been received.
Its validator enforces the phrase-form-only boundary, and the
[phrase-form comparator](scripts/compare_corpus_phrase_form_reviews.py)
preserves disagreements and requires adjudication before inventory or
recurrence denominators change.

The [delivery evidence index](writeups/delivery-evidence-index.md) reconciles
41 selected media items: 29 raw audio/video items, 21 raw items with retained
transcripts, 8 raw items without transcripts, 8 derived-features-only items and
21 internal multimodal observations. Independent delivery observations remain
at zero.
The [18-job delivery sampling queue](writeups/communication-job-delivery-sampling.md)
adds two transcript-aligned candidate samples per job (36 total; 32 with raw
audio/video and four with derived or other media states). Its manual fields are
blank; no vocal, nonverbal, setting or editing findings are inferred.
The queue also includes a typed review schema and
[export validator](scripts/validate_communication_job_delivery_review.py);
validation of a form does not make the observation independent evidence.
The [delivery comparator](scripts/compare_communication_job_delivery_reviews.py)
retains field-level disagreements and requires adjudication before observations
can affect a pattern card.
The [measurement preflight](writeups/communication-job-delivery-measurement-preflight.md)
attaches reproducible proxy values to the queue: 32/36 samples have aligned
feature rows and four are unmatched. RMS, speech activity, pitch, frame and
face-count fields remain measurement proxies pending manual and independent
review.
The [internal midpoint-frame audit](writeups/communication-job-delivery-internal-observations.md)
now records bounded visual/setting observations for all 32 raw audio/video
samples. It explicitly records edited subtitles, cutaways, staged scenes and
off-screen speakers where present; it is not independent review, continuous
video review or manual vocal listening.
The [shared evidence model](writeups/shared-evidence-model.md) formalizes the
web/mobile contract: entity types, claim levels, status vocabulary, provenance
links, missing/disagreement handling and separate learner/listener records. Its
manifest is generated from live artifacts; the contract is ready while the
research gates remain incomplete.
It now includes a counterexample-observation entity and a blank 90-slot
[counterexample workbook](research/communication-job-counterexample-workbook.json):
five required limitation checks for each of the 18 provisional jobs. No slot
has been independently completed.
Its validator now requires source fields for confirmed or candidate entries,
while the [summarizer](scripts/summarize_communication_job_counterexamples.py)
preserves `no_candidate_found` decisions and status counts without promoting
them to outcome evidence.
The [pattern-admission reconciliation](writeups/pattern-admission-reconciliation.md)
now distinguishes the legacy five contextual threshold passes from the eight
shared draft cards; neither count represents independent validation.

The [corpus transcript-derived lesson inventory](writeups/corpus-lesson-index.md)
now exposes the lesson-shaped material already recorded in all 395 full-context
reviews: 4,016 episode-map communication-function items and 2,800 structural
relations, each linked to the review's exact context evidence, limits and next
test. Its [browser](writeups/corpus-lesson-index.html) makes the complete
inventory searchable by title, video ID, function, relation or evidence ID.
This is a transcript-derived discovery index, not a validated pattern library;
it does not establish recurrence, delivery effects, teachability or listener
outcomes.

The [provisional shared communication-job taxonomy](writeups/corpus-function-taxonomy.md)
maps those free-text episode labels to a comparison vocabulary while preserving
the original labels. It currently maps 1,622 items heuristically and leaves
2,394 items explicitly unclassified for human canonical-job review; this layer
is not independent semantic validation.

The [exact-caption evidence bank](writeups/corpus-evidence-bank.md) joins all
4,016 function records to 102,816 retained caption segments, timestamps and
timestamped source URLs. Wording is preserved as stored, including recognition
errors; no transcript repair, delivery inference or outcome claim is added.

The [provisional communication-job cards](writeups/communication-job-cards.md)
turn 18 shared jobs into practice hypotheses with exact examples, recurrence
inventory counts, repeatable forms, self-checks and non-fit warnings. They are
not validated lessons: independent review, delivery checks, learner checks and
listener outcomes remain pending.
Their example selector now ranks lexical speech above marker-only cues such as
music or applause and records the quality metadata without repairing the stored
transcript wording.
Five of the cards also carry 19 linked internal multimodal observations with
frame notes and coarse audio/visual proxies; these remain explicitly
non-independent and do not establish intent, emotion or effectiveness.

The [18-card learner check](writeups/communication-job-learner-check.md) now
provides 72 fixed tasks covering recognition, plain-language explanation,
transfer, non-fit judgment, listener restatement and revision. It is runnable
but has zero learner records and cannot be treated as learning evidence.
Its recognition and explanation tasks now link the 50-form exact inventory
where an indexed family matches; the other ten jobs explicitly remain without
a direct exact-form match rather than receiving invented phrases.
The protocol now also requires facilitator-entered 0–2 rubric scores for six
teachability dimensions per job plus a separate safety/privacy/accessibility
flag. The [non-promotional summarizer](scripts/summarize_communication_job_learner_records.py)
will preserve per-job low scores and safety findings once records exist.
Exports also require facilitator identity/role and a scoring disagreement or
uncertainty note; no facilitator or learner records exist yet.
The integrated [18-job curriculum browser](writeups/communication-job-curriculum.html)
now joins each job's problem, mechanism hypothesis, repeatable form, exact
examples, delivery queue/measurements, counterexample slots, practice,
learner tasks and listener protocol in one provisional surface.
The [mobile lesson payload](writeups/communication-job-mobile-lessons.md)
provides six consent-aware practice steps per job with typed/captioned/
asynchronous options and no required recording; it has zero learner records.

The [18-job listener-outcome protocol](writeups/communication-job-listener-outcome-protocol.md)
defines matched control/pattern-informed stimuli, six comprehension/recall
tasks, required blind 0–2 scores, missingness, accessibility and uncertainty
reporting. The listener record validator rejects unscored task responses, and
the [non-promotional summarizer](scripts/summarize_communication_job_listener_records.py)
reports task distributions by job and condition without promoting them to
general effectiveness evidence.
The [draft stimulus bank](writeups/communication-job-listener-stimuli-draft.md)
now supplies one newly authored pair per job with word-count audits; pairs are
not locked and no listener records exist.
The [automated preflight](writeups/communication-job-stimuli-preflight.md)
checks all 18 pairs for length, token overlap, target markers and safety flags;
all 18 still require manual review, and these checks do not prove equivalence.
The [stimulus review workbook](research/communication-job-listener-stimulus-review-workbook.html)
is now the independent gate for proposition equivalence, readability, target
isolation, accessibility, safety and blinding; all pairs remain unlocked.
The agreement comparator preserves reviewer disagreement and requires explicit
adjudication before any pair can be considered for locking.

The generated [validation handoff](writeups/validation-handoff.md) consolidates
the four independent-review gates plus learner and listener protocols, with
export validators and explicit non-claims; no human records are present.
The [end-to-end acceptance matrix](writeups/end-to-end-acceptance-matrix.md)
maps the definition-of-done requirements to current evidence and reports nine
open requirements; it does not count a workbook or protocol as completed human

The [reproducibility manifest](research/reproducibility-manifest.json) hashes
25 authoritative artifacts and records 395 full-context reviews, 102,816

The [external review gate index](writeups/external-review-gate-index.md) is the
single operational routing page for the ten remaining independent-review,

The [per-job promotion readiness audit](writeups/communication-job-promotion-readiness.md)
now makes the promotion boundary explicit for every one of the 18 provisional

The [next validation runbook](writeups/next-validation-runbook.md) orders the
remaining work for a five-job vertical slice, including dependencies, export

The [vertical-slice reviewer handoff](writeups/vertical-slice-01-reviewer-start-here.md)
is the single entry point for the five packets, 50-item independent workbook,

The atlas also now exposes a separate 187-item [candidate speech-function

It samples up to 12 train and 12 holdout videos for each of eight non-unclassified

The [multimodal coverage audit](research/multimodal-coverage-audit.json)

The [bounded internal multimodal reviews](research/multimodal-expansion-manual-review.json)

The [multimodal independent-review workbook](research/multimodal-independent-review-workbook.json)
now exposes all 21 internally reviewed intervals across both bounded review

preserves field-level disagreements and requires adjudication before any

The [shared provisional curriculum](research/shared-provisional-curriculum.json)
now joins all 18 current communication-job lessons, with links to

The shared lesson-card JSON now exposes an explicit evidence chain for each
card: communication problem, exact language/function, delivery evidence,
mechanism hypothesis, recurrence, limits, practice, learner check and listener
outcome protocol. Missing validation remains represented as status, not inferred
from the presence of links.
All 18 cards now include explicit practice boundaries and self-check fields;
the cards remain provisional rather than implying that the candidate patterns
are proven.
Card examples and controls are also enriched from the retained segment registry
with neighboring timestamped captions when packet context was absent; the
builder does not synthesize missing transcript wording.
Each card now separates the provisional `speech_function` label from the
practice hypothesis, preventing a proposed exercise from being mistaken for an
observed semantic annotation.
The standalone shared-card validator checks this contract independently of the
unit tests. It also carries the contrast packet's existing no-contrast controls,
alternate-function control and ambiguous failure candidate into the card rather
than dropping them; the failure candidate remains explicitly unproven.

The consolidated [end-to-end status dashboard](research/end-to-end-status.json)
and [human-readable status](writeups/end-to-end-status.md) now report the
completion boundary across inventory, transcript reading, retrieval, function

The lesson-card browser now exposes the same chain directly: exact examples,

The [dashboard validator](scripts/validate_end_to_end_status.py) recomputes its

The separate [listener-outcome protocol](research/listener-outcome-protocol.json)
and [record form](writeups/listener-outcome-record.html) define matched

accessibility and blinded scoring. They are designed but not run; no listener
outcome evidence is present.

The five-pattern slice is structurally assembled: packets, linked lessons,
shared practice flows, source/event metadata audit and learner protocol are
present. The [validation audit](writeups/vertical-slice-01-validation-audit.md)
reports 0/5 packets ready for promotion. Independent review, verified
speaker/event independence, manual visual alignment and learner observations
remain open.
Targeted official-source checks now partially corroborate one Jaishankar
speaker/topic record and one Trudeau speaker/context record, while explicitly
leaving exact upload/event correspondence unverified.

The slice also has a browser-based independent-review form for all 50 packet
occurrences and a fixed 20-task learner bank. The learner form records exact
task IDs, baseline/transfer/non-fit/control responses, listener restatement and
revision. These are runnable instruments, not review or learner results.
The review form exposes each item’s timestamp and source link for direct
evidence checking.
The lesson index passes static link checks. Its Playwright render smoke test
passes when the required browser libraries are supplied in user space (five
cards, navigation links, filtering, labeled search control and 390px mobile
layout. It also checks that the learner instrument renders five pattern
sections, consent, fixed task IDs and its download control on mobile; the base
environment still lacks `libnspr4.so`, so the default invocation reports that
dependency explicitly. With temporary user-space browser libraries, the smoke
test also exercises the required-field form and verifies a synthetic exported
learner record’s type, consent and task-bank fields.
Exported independent-review records now have a machine validator that checks
the independence attestation, all 50 item fields, controlled decisions and
required rationale fields without endorsing the judgments.
Validated exports can now be summarized by pattern and item role; the summary
remains explicitly non-promotional and preserves indeterminate decisions.
Two or more validated exports can now be compared for item-level agreement and
disagreement; this report also remains non-promotional until adjudication.
The media audit also reconciles 50 packet occurrences across 16 video IDs and
now finds fifteen IDs with reacquired raw audio/video and one ID still missing
raw media because the source is unavailable. All 16 IDs have derived feature
files, and media-status records now agree with local file presence; derived
features remain separate from manual multimodal evidence.

not independently verified human review. Next: expand contextual re-review to
single-label cases, then evaluate localized audio/video evidence and matched controls.

This file is the practical handoff for resuming the project. The canonical
long-form goal remains in
[`SPEECH_ANALYSIS_END_TO_END_GOAL.md`](SPEECH_ANALYSIS_END_TO_END_GOAL.md).

## The meaty end-to-end goal

Given the three supplied speech and presentation playlists—and additional
relevant playlists added later—build a large, auditable research corpus
covering every discoverable video in those playlists. Use the corpus to
determine how effective speakers repeatedly organize ideas, use language,
control timing, use their voice, move their face and body, interact with
slides, answer questions, disagree, tell stories, and guide an audience's
attention.

The final result should be more than summaries or a list of presentation
tips. It should be a tested explanatory account of recurring communication
patterns. For every proposed pattern, show:

- where it occurs, with video IDs and timestamps;
- what was directly observed or measured;
- what speech function or presentation context was taking place;
- whether it recurs across speakers, videos, formats, and topics;
- what contradicts or qualifies the pattern;
- what alternative explanations may exist; and
- what a person could practice, with the evidence and limits made clear.

The unit of analysis is a timestamped speech segment linked across channels:
transcript words, audio delivery, visible face and body behavior, slides or
scene context, and the surrounding speech function. The project must separate
measurement from interpretation. A pause, pitch change, speaking-rate change,
facial movement, gaze direction, gesture, or posture change can be recorded as
an observation; confidence, emotion, intent, persuasion, or comprehension may
only be discussed as a cautious hypothesis unless independently supported.

The corpus is playlist-scale. The three URLs below are seed playlists, not
three sample videos. The intended inventory is every eligible video in each
playlist, deduplicated across playlists, with unavailable or inaccessible
items explicitly recorded. The project should eventually compare speaker-
specific, situation-specific, and cross-speaker patterns rather than treating
one repeated behavior as a universal best practice.

## Seed playlists

- [Learn English with Speeches](https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLosaC3gb0kGDhmBVm6M47jcU8BbEhTnlP)
- [Greatest Movie Speeches](https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLLVLl07BgiJYSCSdl1fd34s4SzILbMxp9)
- [The Best Speeches Ever](https://www.youtube.com/playlist?list=PLosaC3gb0kGDEFRm7OxnWgOmJkEki1LPj)

## Verified current status

### Corpus and transcript layer

- 958 playlist memberships were inventoried.
- 480 unique videos remain after deduplication.
- 478 duplicate memberships were recorded in the inventory.
- One unavailable playlist item was hidden during enumeration.
- 477 transcript responses were attempted through ScrapeCreators.
- 474 responses succeeded; 3 returned provider 404s:
  `BS8I9H07wKw`, `E7Sr-cpFwUc`, and `nWRxPDhd3d0`.
- 395 videos have non-empty transcripts; 82 successful responses have zero
  transcript segments.
- The corpus contains 102,816 timestamped transcript segments and 770,424
  words.
- Transcript acquisition uses the root `/home/mehtama1/git-repo/.env`.
  Credentials must never be printed, committed, or copied into this repo.

### Multimodal media and feature layer

- The phase-two multimodal sample contains 41 videos: 24 pilot videos and 17
  additional videos selected across duration buckets.
- 36 videos have complete audio/video pairs; 5 have media failures.
- Audio features contain 19,233 one-second windows and 5,059 transcript-aligned
  segments across those 36 videos.
- Current audio measurements include RMS/loudness, a speech-activity proxy,
  zero-crossing rate, spectral centroid, coarse spectral pitch and pitch
  confidence proxies, transcript word counts, words-per-second proxies, and
  transcript gaps before and after segments.
- Visual features currently record sampled frames, face presence and geometry
  through OpenCV Haar detection, and scene-pixel measures. They do not infer
  emotion.
- Raw audio and video are intentionally ignored by Git. Manifests, derived
  features, status records, and evidence are tracked instead.

### Body and movement layer

- Pose features cover the same 36 multimodal videos with four evenly spaced
  frames per video: 144 sampled frames total.
- A person was detected in 104 of those frames.
- The current layer records observable person/keypoint geometry only.
- Frame-to-frame movement analysis produced 68 movement pairs and 352
  transcript/motion retrieval joins.
- Current movement metrics include overall keypoint, wrist, and shoulder
  displacement. Cuts, zooms, camera movement, tracking errors, and framing
  can create apparent movement, so these are exploratory measurements.
- MediaPipe was not retained because of dependency incompatibilities. The
  current pose implementation uses the pinned torchvision approach documented
  in `requirements-vision.txt`.

### Human-reviewed evidence layer

- `research/annotation-ledger.jsonl` contains 87 reviewed annotations.
- The latest audit identifies 62 supported annotation rows; some rows carry
  more than one candidate label.
- Current reviewed support rates are:

  | Candidate pattern | Supported / reviewed | Support rate |
  | --- | ---: | ---: |
  | call to action | 7 / 11 | 0.636 |
  | conclusion or summary | 5 / 6 | 0.833 |
  | contrast or disagreement | 9 / 10 | 0.900 |
  | definition | 7 / 9 | 0.778 |
  | example | 8 / 10 | 0.800 |
  | pause event | 2 / 7 | 0.286 |
  | question | 7 / 12 | 0.583 |
  | story or personal experience | 10 / 13 | 0.769 |
  | uncertainty or qualification | 9 / 9 | 1.000 |

- Six provisional practice cards currently pass the admission rule of at
  least five supported examples, four distinct videos, and a support rate of
  at least 0.70: contrast/disagreement, definition, example,
  story/personal-experience, uncertainty/qualification, and
  conclusion/summary.
- Question and pause cards are deliberately excluded from the provisional
  practice edition because their current support rates are too low.
- The cards include direct measurements, timestamped examples, counterexamples,
  limits, visual-evidence status, and a required next test. They are not claims
  that any behavior is universally effective.

## What is complete and what is not

The inventory and transcript acquisition foundation is in place, and the
repository has a working auditable pipeline from playlist membership through
transcripts, derived audio/visual/pose measurements, reviewed annotations,
counterexamples, and provisional reader-facing pattern cards.

The research is not finished. In particular:

- The multimodal media sample covers 36 complete videos, not all 480 unique
  videos.
- Manual review is still a small, structured sample, not full reading and
  annotation of every transcript.
- Speech-function labels and transcript/audio joins are partly heuristic and
  need broader review.
- Pose coverage is four sampled frames per video; it is not continuous gesture
  tracking.
- Facial expression, gaze, gesture semantics, audience response, speaker
  identity, and slide understanding are not yet reliable measured layers.
- There is no causal evidence that any observed behavior improves persuasion,
  comprehension, or presentation outcomes.
- The project must not state that a speaker is confident, deceptive, nervous,
  intelligent, honest, emotional, or persuasive as a directly observed fact.
- Provider failures, empty transcripts, media failures, editing, microphones,
  compression, music, topic, and camera framing remain important limitations.

## Resumption point

Start from the repository root:

```bash
cd /home/mehtama1/git-repo/best-speech-analysis
git status --short --branch
git pull --ff-only origin master
```

Useful validation and report rebuild commands:

```bash
python3 scripts/audit_transcript_coverage.py
python3 scripts/audit_annotation_ledger.py
python3 scripts/build_reviewed_delivery_report.py
python3 scripts/compare_reviewed_delivery_baselines.py
python3 scripts/build_provisional_practice_patterns.py
python3 scripts/analyze_pose_geometry.py
python3 scripts/analyze_pose_movement.py
```

Do not rerun the expensive pose extractor or redownload the corpus unless the
next task specifically requires it. The ScrapeCreators credential is available
from the parent `.env` through the existing scripts; keep it outside tracked
files and do not expose it in logs.

## Recommended next work

1. Continue human review from the remaining annotation queue, deliberately
   balancing speakers, uploaders, speech functions, formats, and video lengths.
2. Expand the multimodal sample and media recovery, recording every failure and
   selection rule in manifests.
3. Inspect pose and visual evidence against timestamped transcript functions,
   while keeping camera/framing confounds explicit.
4. Improve metadata for speaker, format, prepared versus interview speech,
   audience setting, editing, and slide presence.
5. Add a robust facial-landmark or expression measurement path only after its
   dependencies and validation plan are compatible with this environment.
6. Rebuild pattern cards only when cross-video support, counterexamples, and
   direct evidence justify them; then produce the reader/practice edition.

## Research rules

- Preserve source IDs, timestamps, provenance, and retrieval status for every
  claim and example.
- Distinguish measured observation, interpretation, and practical suggestion.
- Prefer counterexamples and uncertainty over inflated generalizations.
- Do not infer protected or private traits, mental states, honesty, or intent.
- Do not commit raw media, credentials, or unrestricted copied source material.
- Treat automated transcripts, audio proxies, visual detectors, and model
  outputs as fallible evidence requiring quality checks.
